{"antonyms":[{"word":"non-destructive read"}],"forms":[{"form":"destructive reads","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Although ferroelectric RAM has many advantages over conventional DRAM, it does share one of DRAM's disadvantages: as with DRAM, reading a FeRAM cell is a destructive read process which erases any data in the cell.
